44.28 Related parties                                                                      44.28 關聯方
      A party is considered to be related to the Company if:                                     在下列情況下，一方被視為與本公司有關連：

     (a)   the party is a person or a close member of that person’s family and                  (a)    該方為個人或該個人之近親，而該個人
           that person

           (i)     has control or joint control over the Company;                                      (i)     對本公司控制或共同控制；

           (ii)    has significant influence over the Company; or                                      (ii)    對本公司具有重大影響；或

           (iii)   is a member of the key management personnel of the Company                          (iii)   為本公司或本公司之母公司之主要
                   or of a parent of the Company;                                                              管理層人員；

     Or                                                                                         或

     (b)   the party is an entity where any of the following conditions applies:                (b)    該方為符合以下任何情況之實體：

           (i)     the entity and the Company are members of the same group;                           (i)     該實體與本公司為同一集團之成員；

           (ii)    one entity is an associate or joint venture of the other entity (or                 (ii)    該實體為另一實體（或該另一實體之
                   of a parent, subsidiary or fellow subsidiary of the other entity);                          母公司、附屬公司或同系附屬公司）
                                                                                                               之聯營公司或合營公司；

           (iii)   the entity and the Company are joint ventures of the same third                     (iii)   該實體與本公司為同一第三方之合
                   party;                                                                                      營公司；

           (iv)    one entity is a joint venture of a third entity and the other entity                (iv)    某 實 體 為 第 三 方 之 合 營 公 司，而 另
                   is an associate of the third entity;                                                        一實體為該第三方之聯營公司；

           (v)     the entity is controlled or jointly controlled by a person identified               (v)     該 實 體 由(a)中 所 示 之 個 人 控 制 或 共
                   in (a);                                                                                     同控制；

           (vi)    a person identified in (a)(i) has significant influence over the                    (vi)    (a)(i)中所示之個人對該實體具有重大
                   entity or is a member of the key management personnel of the                                影響，或其為該實體（或該實體之母
                   entity (or of a parent of the entity); and                                                  公司）主要管理人員；及

           (vii) the entity, or any member of a group of which it is a part,                           (vii)   該實體或該實體所在集團之任何成
                 provides key management personnel services to the Company or                                  員，為 本 公 司 或 其 母 公 司 提 供 主 要
                 to the parent of the Company.                                                                 管理人員服務。

 44.29 Events after the reporting period                                              44.29 報告期後事項
       If the Company receives information after the reporting period, but prior            倘本公司在報告期後但在授權刊發日期之前接
       to the date of authorisation for issue, about conditions that existed at the         獲有關於報告期末存在的條件的資料，其將評
       end of the reporting period, it will assess whether the information affects          估該資料是否影響其在財務報表確認的金額。
       the amounts that it recognises in its financial statements. The Company              本公司將調整在財務報表中確認的金額，以反
       will adjust the amounts recognised in its financial statements to reflect            映報告期後的任何調整事項，並根據新資料更
       any adjusting events after the reporting period and update the disclosures           新與該等條件相關的披露。就報告期後非調整
       that relate to those conditions in light of the new information. For non-            事項而言，本公司將不會變更財務報表中確認
       adjusting events after the reporting period, the Company will not change the         的金額，但將披露非調整事項的性質及其財務
       amounts recognised in its financial statements, but will disclose the nature         影響的估計，或作出無法進行估計的聲明（如適
       of the non-adjusting events and an estimate of their financial effects, or a         用）。
       statement that such an estimate cannot be made, if applicable.
